A single forward pass over the clean latent exposes scene content beyond current observations, including occluded and out-of-sight hands. We introduce DreamHand, an offline clip-level framework that extracts features via a Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder and decodes them with a Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder. DreamHand recovers continuous bimanual trajectories with metric placement and no external detector, while a Ray-Based Camera Solver supports a second configuration that needs no test-time camera intrinsics. Across five egocentric benchmarks, DreamHand sets a new state of the art, cutting MPJPE-p by 30% on occlusion-heavy ARCTIC and 40% on HOT3D. These gains reach 46%-61% once out-of-sight hands are included in the evaluation, offering a scalable path from everyday human video to robot manipulation data.

</details>


### [63] [Inter-X++: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Human-Human Interaction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20312)
*Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Zili Lin,Xintao Lv,Yichao Yan,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Inter-X++，一个大规模、高保真的人-人交互（HHI）基准数据集，包含 11,388 个交互序列（超 810 万帧）和丰富的多模态标注；并在此基础上提出统一表示与建模框架 OpenHHI，在生成与感知两类任务上均达到最先进性能，证明统一的交互表示能够同时桥接交互理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HHI 数据集与建模方法受限于三大瓶颈：低保真运动学数据、缺少灵巧手部动作，以及多模态标注严重匮乏；同时，碎片化的交互表示和不一致的评测协议阻碍了公平、严谨的基准测试。这些缺陷使得当前数字人系统难以真正感知和合成自然的人-人交互。

Method: 采用新型混合动作捕捉系统采集高保真数据，提供精确的全身运动与手指关节细节；为数据补充多层细粒度文本描述、交互类别、因果交互顺序、主体关系与性格、顶点级接触图及物理正则化约束等多维度标注；基于这些标注构建覆盖生成与感知两大范式的四类下游任务统一测试平台，并系统标准化交互表示与评测协议；最后提出 OpenHHI 统一表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建与语义理解。

Result: Inter-X++ 提供 11,388 个高保真交互序列与超过 810 万帧数据，包含完整身体运动和手指精细关节；OpenHHI 在生成与感知任务上均取得最先进（SOTA）性能，验证了统一表示能够有效连接交互理解与生成。

Conclusion: Inter-X++ 系统性地解决了 HHI 研究中的数据瓶颈、表示碎片化和评测不一致问题；OpenHHI 的实验结果最终证明，统一的交互表示可以同时支撑理解与生成，为智能数字人系统的发展奠定坚实基础。

Abstract: The capability to perceive and synthesize human-human interactions is fundamental to developing intelligent digital human systems. However, existing datasets and modeling approaches are fundamentally constrained by low-fidelity kinematics, the omission of dexterous hand gestures and a severe lack of rich multimodal annotations. Furthermore, fragmented interaction representations and inconsistent evaluation protocols also impede fair and rigorous benchmarking. To systematically address these bottlenecks, we present Inter-X++, a comprehensive and large-scale benchmark designed to empower versatile HHI analysis. Captured via a novel hybrid motion capture system, Inter-X++ provides 11,388 high-fidelity interaction sequences and over 8.1M frames, featuring precise whole-body movements and detailed finger articulations. Meanwhile, we enrich the data foundation with multifaceted annotations, including hierarchical fine-grained textual descriptions, interaction categories, causal interaction orders, the relationship and personality of the subjects, as well as vertex-level contact maps and physically regularized constraints. Leveraging these elaborate annotations, we formulate a unified testing ground comprising four categories of downstream tasks that symmetrically span both generative and perceptive paradigms. To eliminate benchmarking ambiguities, we systematically standardize the interaction representations and evaluation protocols. Finally, we go beyond dataset construction to propose OpenHHI, a single and unified HHI representation and modeling framework that jointly optimizes interaction reconstruction and semantic understanding. Extensive experiments reveal that OpenHHI achieves state-of-the-art performance on both generation and perception tasks. This definitively proves that our unified representation successfully bridges interaction understanding and generation simultaneously.

</details>


### [64] [Swift-Image: Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334)
*Taihang Hu,Zhao Wang,Zuan Gao,Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Zhengze Xu,Yuhang Yu,Yongchao Du,Xingjian Wang,Jun Zheng,Qinye Zhou,Zhengrui Chen,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Zhengtao Wu,Ge Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Denghui Yang,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Swift-Image 是一个紧凑的统一模型（6B 单流 DiT），同时支持文生图、单图编辑和多图编辑，通过系统的训练工程（渐进式训练管线、并行专家强化学习、多教师在线蒸馏、提示增强器、结构化剪枝与少步蒸馏）在受限算力预算（243K GPU 小时）下达到开源模型中的领先聚合性能，并产出近乎无损的 3B 压缩版与加速推理变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在受限计算预算下，一个相对较小的视觉生成模型能通过系统性训练工程被推进到何种程度，从而在文本到图像生成、单图编辑与多图编辑等异构任务上实现统一且高性能的生成能力，并总结可复用的工程经验。

Method: 采用高效的 6B 单流 DiT 架构；训练使用渐进式管线，从广泛语义覆盖逐步过渡到更高分辨率、更强视觉质量和统一的生成-编辑监督。后训练阶段采用并行专家强化学习、随后进行多教师在线蒸馏以缓解异构目标间的干扰。将高层推理与像素级渲染解耦，引入 Prompt Enhancer 将用户请求翻译为生成器对齐的视觉规格。部署时使用结构化剪枝与少步蒸馏，产出 3B 与加速变体。

Result: Swift-Image 以仅 6B 参数和 243K GPU 训练小时在评估的开源模型中取得领先聚合性能；压缩后的 3B 模型性能几乎无损，少步蒸馏在显著减少采样步数的同时进一步提升聚合编辑性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过合理的架构选择、数据课程设计、后训练策略、提示增强与模型压缩，小型统一生成模型能够在有限算力下匹敌甚至超越更大模型的聚合表现；论文同时总结了关于架构、数据课程、后训练、提示增强与模型压缩的实用经验教训。

Abstract: We present Swift-Image, a compact unified model for text-to-image generation, single-image editing, and multi-image editing. Our goal is to explore how far a relatively small visual generator can be pushed through systematic training engineering under a constrained computational budget. Swift-Image adopts an efficient 6B single-stream DiT and a progressive training pipeline that evolves from broad semantic coverage to higher resolution, stronger visual quality, and unified generation-editing supervision. For post-training, we employ parallel expert reinforcement learning followed by multi-teacher on-policy distillation to alleviate interference among heterogeneous objectives. We further decouple high-level reasoning from pixel-level rendering with a Prompt Enhancer that translates user requests into generator-aligned visual specifications. For efficient deployment, structural pruning and few-step distillation produce 3B and accelerated variants. Swift-Image achieves leading aggregate performance among evaluated open-source models with only 6B parameters and 243K GPU training hours; the compressed 3B model incurs nearly no loss, while few-step distillation further improves aggregate editing performance with substantially fewer sampling steps. Our study also summarizes practical lessons for architecture, data curriculum, post-training, prompt enhancement, and model compression.

</details>


### [65] [4DAnyone: Create Anyone in 4D from a Casual Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20335)
*Yudong Jin,Tao Xie,Qihang Zhang,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DAnyone 是一个从无标定单目视频重建 4D 人类的方法框架：先利用相机可控的视频扩散模型生成重建级的多视角一致视频，再将其提升为 4D 高斯泼溅（4DGS）。针对目标视角数超出单次 DiT 前向容量而导致的一致性崩溃问题，论文将其归结为“有界注意力上下文”问题，并提出参考上下文打包（RCP）与目标上下文路由（TCR）两种互补设计，分别以 O(1) 复杂度压缩历史参考视图、并在去噪过程中轮转目标视图分组以共享跨组上下文。配合自建 MVGameHuman 数据集与光场/野外视频数据训练，该方法在 DNA-Rendering 与 DyMVHumans 上的新视角视频质量和 4DGS 重建均优于先前方法，并具备鲁棒的野外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相机可控视频扩散模型可以合成看似合理的新视角视频，但当目标视角扩展到 4DGS 重建所需的数十个视角时，视频之间的多视角一致性无法保持。作者将这一失败归因于“有界注意力上下文”问题：目标视角超过单次 DiT 前向传播容量时必须分组，进而暴露两个耦合瓶颈——参考上下文侧（对全部历史生成视图的条件化规模为 O(N)，削弱跨视图外观引导）与目标上下文侧（互不相交的分组无法直接交换信息，导致全局结构漂移）。

Method: 方法包含两大互补设计：(1) 参考上下文打包（Reference Context Packing, RCP）：将不断增长的参考视图压缩成固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，使参考上下文复杂度从 O(N) 降为 O(1)；(2) 目标上下文路由（Target Context Routing, TCR）：在去噪过程中轮转目标视图的分组方式，高噪声步时跨组共享上下文以维护全局一致性，低噪声步时稳定细节。训练上构建了基于内部游戏引擎的 MVGameHuman 数据集，并与光场（light-stage）和野外（in-the-wild）视频数据集联合训练。最终将生成的视频提升为 4D 高斯泼溅（4DGS）。

Result: 在 DNA-Rendering 和 DyMVHumans 基准上的实验表明，4DAnyone 在新视角视频质量以及下游 4DGS 重建任务上均优于先前方法，并展现出鲁棒的野外（in-the-wild）泛化能力。

Conclusion: 4DAnyone 通过 RCP 与 TCR 两种互补机制有效解决了大规模目标视角下视频扩散模型的一致性崩溃问题，从而实现了从单目无标定视频到高质量 4DGS 重建的完整流程，并在基准测试和野外场景中验证了其优越性与泛化性。

Abstract: We present 4DAnyone, a framework for reconstructing 4D humans from an uncalibrated monocular video by generating reconstruction-grade multiview-consistent videos and lifting them into 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS). Existing camera-controlled video diffusion models synthesize plausible novel-view videos but fail to maintain consistency when scaled to the tens of target views required for 4DGS reconstruction. We identify this failure as a bounded-attention-context problem: when target views exceed the capacity of a single DiT forward pass, they must be split into groups, exposing two coupled bottlenecks. On the reference-context side, conditioning on all previously generated views grows as $O(N)$, weakening cross-view appearance guidance. On the target-context side, disjoint groups cannot directly exchange information, causing global structural drift. 4DAnyone addresses both bottlenecks with two complementary designs: Reference Context Packing (RCP) compresses growing reference views into a fixed-length mixed-resolution context with $O(1)$ reference-context complexity, while Target Context Routing (TCR) rotates target-view groupings during denoising to share context across groups at high-noise steps and stabilize details at low-noise steps. We further build the MVGameHuman dataset using our in-house game engine and combine it with light-stage and in-the-wild video datasets for training. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and DyMVHumans show that 4DAnyone outperforms prior methods in both novel-view video quality and downstream 4DGS reconstruction, with robust in-the-wild generalization. See our project page for video results and source code: https://4danyone.github.io.

</details>


### [66] [WithEveryone: Unified Planning and Identity Grounding for Group Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20336)
*Hengyuan Xu,Qixun Wang,Yiji Cheng,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Wei Cheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WithEveryone 是一个统一的框架，用于生成最多包含十位指定身份人物的群像图像。它通过将每个身份注入为寻址令牌、预测结构化的身份-布局计划并将其渲染为视觉条件，结合布局锚定的身份损失（Layout-Grounded ID Loss）和身份表示强制（ID Representation Forcing），实现了高保真度的多身份图像生成，显著提升了身份相似度并大幅减少了复制-粘贴伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的身份保持图像生成方法在处理包含多个指定人物的场景时可靠性急剧下降，模型需要在保留每个身份的同时，将每个参考身份绑定到不同的具体人物和空间位置，而训练时的身份损失函数必须建立多个噪声预测人脸之间的对应关系，这导致多身份生成面临巨大挑战。

Method: WithEveryone 框架包含三个核心组成部分：(1) 将每个选定身份注入为寻址令牌（addressed token）；(2) 预测结构化的身份-布局计划（identity–layout plan）；(3) 将该计划渲染为视觉条件。关键目标函数是布局锚定的身份损失（Layout-Grounded ID Loss），利用标注的人脸区域直接监督目标身份，避免了不稳定的基于嵌入的人脸匹配；此外还采用身份表示强制（ID Representation Forcing），在图像合成前为每个身份训练预测。

Result: 在身份分离的基准测试上，WithEveryone 取得了最高的目标-上下文身份相似度，将人脸相似度从 GPT-Image-2 的 0.462 提升至 0.499，同时将复制-粘贴伪影从 0.169 降低至 0.055。此外，它覆盖了 97.3% 的请求身份，重复率仅为 2.8%。

Conclusion: 显式的身份-布局锚定使得身份保持生成能够扩展到更大的群体，而无需依赖直接的参考人脸复制。这表明布局引导的身份损失策略是实现高保真度、可扩展的多人物生成的有效途径。

Abstract: Identity-preserving image generation becomes increasingly unreliable when a scene must contain many specified people. Beyond retaining each identity, the model must bind every reference to a distinct person and location, while training-time identity losses must establish correspondence among several noisy predicted faces. We introduce WithEveryone, a unified framework for generating group images up to ten reference identities. WithEveryone injects each selected identity as an addressed token, predicts a structured identity--layout plan, and renders the plan as a visual condition. Its key objective, Layout-Grounded ID Loss, uses annotated face regions to supervise the intended identities directly, avoiding unstable embedding-based face matching; ID Representation Forcing additionally trains a prediction for each identity before image synthesis. On an identity-disjoint benchmark, WithEveryone achieves the highest target-context identity similarity, improving face similarity from 0.462 for GPT-Image-2 to 0.499, while reducing copy-paste artifacts from 0.169 to 0.055. It further covers 97.3\% of the requested identities with a duplicate rate of only 2.8\%. These results show that explicit identity--layout grounding enables identity-preserving generation to scale to larger groups without relying on direct reference-face copying.

</details>

### [67] [A Virtual Member of a Community of Practice for the Society of Petroleum Engineers: From Prototype to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19199)
*John Boden,Joshua Eckroth,Dayne Freitag,Skyler Gipson,Johnathan Keefe,Karen Myers,Eric Schoen,Pedro Sequeira,Reid Smith,Michael Wessel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为ATHENA的虚拟助手的演进过程，该助手旨在支持石油天然气行业实践社区（CoP）成员的知识捕获、检索和传播。经过对75名专业人士的评估，第一代原型相比最先进的RAG基线系统显著提高了生产力和绩效平等性，但仍有改进空间。后续技术改进包括多文档检索、答案验证支持和更主动的针对性知识传播，增强版在知识密集型任务上表现更优。ATHENA已集成到SPE研究门户并部署供会员使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为石油天然气行业实践社区（CoP）成员提供更好的知识管理支持，通过虚拟助手提升知识捕获、检索和传播的效率，解决传统RAG系统在知识密集型任务中的不足。

Method: 开发了一个名为ATHENA的虚拟助手，第一代原型基于RAG系统；通过评估发现不足后，进行了三项技术改进：多文档检索、支持答案验证、更主动的针对性知识传播。最终将系统集成到SPE研究门户中。

Result: 第一代原型在75名专业人士的评估中，相比最先进的RAG基线系统显著提高了生产力和绩效平等性。增强版本在知识密集型井规划任务上提供比基线系统更好的支持。ATHENA已成功集成到SPE研究门户并部署使用。

Conclusion: ATHENA虚拟助手通过技术演进，有效提升了石油天然气行业实践社区的知识管理水平，增强版在检索、验证和主动传播方面的改进使其在知识密集型任务中优于现有基线系统，实际部署验证了其应用价值。

Abstract: We describe the evolution of a virtual assistant, called ATHENA, designed to support the capture, retrieval, and dissemination of knowledge for members of a Community of Practice (CoP) related to the Oil and Gas sector. An evaluation of a first prototype involving 75 professionals from the Society of Petroleum Engineering (SPE) showed that ATHENA dramatically improved both their productivity and performance equality on a set of realistic well-planning tasks compare to their use of a state-of-the-art RAG baseline system. However, the evaluation also identified areas for improvement. This paper describes technical advances to our first prototype in the areas of multi-document retrieval, support for answer validation, and more focused proactive dissemination. Evaluation results show that this enhanced version of ATHENA provides better support for completing knowledge-intensive tasks related to well planning than does a state-of-the-art baseline. ATHENA has been integrated into the SPE Research Portal and is being deployed for use by the society's membership.

</details>


### [68] [Transformer Models for Text Summarization: A Comparative Study of BART, BERT, and RoBERTa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19200)
*Daisy Aptovska,Vinayak Elangovan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对现代自动文本摘要技术的聚焦综述，重点回顾基于Transformer的模型和大语言模型（BERT、RoBERTa、BART），分析其架构、预训练策略及在抽取式与生成式摘要中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文本摘要技术近年来随着NLP发展快速演进，但现有综述缺乏对以Transformer/LLM为核心的现代摘要方法的系统梳理，需专门聚焦BERT、RoBERTa、BART等代表性模型进行深入分析。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，按输入类型（单文档/多文档）和输出类型（抽取式/生成式/混合式）对ATS方法分类，重点剖析Transformer架构（BERT、RoBERTa、BART）的设计原理、预训练目标及它们在抽取式与生成式摘要任务中的适配机制。

Result: 系统梳理了BERT、RoBERTa、BART的架构差异：BERT和RoBERTa适合抽取式摘要（基于编码器表示进行句子选择），BART作为序列到序列模型更适合生成式摘要；同时指出现代ATS方法正从纯抽取式向混合式和生成式方向发展。

Conclusion: Transformer/LLM已成为文本摘要的主流技术基石，不同架构（编码器-only vs. 序列到序列）对应不同的摘要范式；未来研究可进一步探索LLM在长文档摘要、多文档摘要及跨语言摘要中的潜力。

Abstract: Text summarization refers to the task of condensing a document into a shorter version while preserving its key information. Automatic text summarization (ATS), driven by advancements in natural language processing (NLP), has developed rapidly in recent years. ATS methods are commonly categorized by input type (such as single-document or multi-document summarization) and by output type (extractive, abstractive, and hybrid). This article presents a focused review of modern summarization techniques with an emphasis on transformer based models and large language models (LLMs), specifically BERT, RoBERTa and BART. It examines their architectures, pretraining strategies, and their suitability for extractive and abstractive summarization tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Automatic bioinformatic software named entity recognition from literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19201)
*Hao Xuan,Rithvij Pasupuleti,Ben Liu,Haishuo Sun,Jun Zhang,Zijun Yao,Cuncong Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SNAIL，一个用于从生物医学文献中自动识别生物信息学软件与数据库名称的混合命名实体识别框架，融合基于拼写模式与上下文线索的词汇策略，以及基于 SciBERT 等 transformer 嵌入和显式 token 掩码的语义策略，在多个基准与真实文献上显著超越现有方法（包括 bioNerDS2 及 ChatGPT、Gemini 等通用大模型），并支撑大规模文献分析与元研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学软件与数据库是现代生命科学研究的关键组成部分，但它们在科学文献中的提及往往不一致，难以大规模系统识别；缺乏全面且更新的生物信息学资源目录，阻碍了自动化生物医学知识抽取和数据分析流程的推进。

Method: SNAIL 采用混合命名实体识别框架：词汇组件捕获软件/数据库名称特有的正字法模式与上下文线索；语义组件利用 transformer（如 SciBERT）生成的上下文嵌入，并结合显式 token 掩码策略增强实体聚焦表示。训练语料通过混合流水线自动构建，包括引文提示抽取与大型语言模型辅助蒸馏。评估在两组独立基准数据集和真实研究文章上进行，并应用于大规模文献分析。

Result: 在独立基准与真实文献上，SNAIL 显著优于现有方法，包括领域专用方法 bioNerDS2 以及 ChatGTP、Gemini、Grok、Claude 等通用大语言模型；将其应用于大规模文献分析还揭示了不同生物信息学子领域在期刊层面的工具使用偏好。

Conclusion: SNAIL 为在科学文本中识别生物信息学资源提供了准确且可扩展的解决方案，能够实现工具使用与研究趋势的系统性元分析。

Abstract: Bioinformatics software and databases are essential components of modern life science research, yet their mentions in the scientific literature are often inconsistent and difficult to systematically identify at scale. The lack of a comprehensive and up-to-date catalog of bioinformatics resources hinders efforts toward automated biomedical knowledge extraction and streamlined data analysis. Here we present SNAIL, a hybrid named entity recognition framework designed to automatically identify bioinformatics software and database (SW/DB) names from biomedical texts. SNAIL integrates complementary lexical and semantic modeling strategies. The lexical component captures orthographic patterns and contextual cues characteristic of SW/DB names, while the semantic component leverages contextual embeddings generated by transformer-based language models such as SciBERT, combined with an explicit token-masking strategy to enhance entity-focused representations. A large training corpus was constructed automatically through a hybrid pipeline that integrates citation-hinted extraction with large language model-assisted distillation. Evaluation on two independent benchmark datasets and real-world research articles demonstrates that SNAIL substantially outperforms existing approaches, including domain-specific methods such as bioNerDS2 and general-purpose large language models such as ChatGPT, Gemini, Grok and Claude. Applying SNAIL to large-scale literature analysis further reveals distinct journal-level preferences across bioinformatics subfields. These results demonstrate that SNAIL provides an accurate and scalable solution for identifying bioinformatics resources in scientific texts and enables systematic meta-analysis of tool usage and research trends.

</details>


### [70] [Asymmetric Attention Heads: Structured Head-Wise Context Allocation for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19203)
*Zimu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出非对称注意力头（AAH），将上下文长度视为每个注意力头/头组的可分配变量，利用特征统计对头进行分组、层次化组织，并为各头分配因果局部窗口，同时保持标准扁平MHA输出接口。在4096-token seed-0实验中，多种AAH式局部分配变体取得了比纯全注意力更低的验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头注意力赋予每个头相同的完整因果上下文范围，但不同头实际承担不同的上下文角色：有些头依赖邻近词法与句法线索，有些头则依赖实体交互、话语连接或状态变化等长距离关系。将上下文长度统一分配在容量和质量上都存在浪费，因此需要一种按头（head-wise）显式分配上下文长度的机制。

Method: AAH将上下文长度作为每个头或每个头组的显式分配变量：(1) 用基于特征的统计量对注意力头进行分组；(2) 对这些组进行层次化组织；(3) 为各组分配因果局部窗口，同时保留标准扁平MHA的输出接口，因此可直接替换现有MHA模块。

Result: 在4096-token seed-0实验中，若干AAH式局部分配变体的验证损失低于纯全注意力基线；短预算消融实验表明：稳定的局部分配策略与头-窗口分配结构对效果至关重要，而固定/局部对照在某些情况下可与自适应层次化方案竞争。论文还报告了注意力覆盖率（ACR）作为选定窗口路由的诊断指标。

Conclusion: AAH是一种结构化、按头分配上下文注意力的机制，兼顾模型质量与可分析性：通过头级窗口分配实现局部性强、路由可诊断的注意力，并在一定配置下优于全注意力基线，为多头注意力的上下文分配提供了一种新的设计空间与诊断工具。

Abstract: Standard multi-head attention (MHA) gives every head the same full causal context span, although heads can serve different contextual roles. Some heads may rely mainly on nearby lexical or syntactic context, while others may depend on longer-range relations such as entity interactions, discourse links, or state changes. We present Asymmetric Attention Heads (AAH), a head-wise context- allocation framework that treats context length as an explicit per-head or per-group allocation variable. AAH groups heads using feature-derived statistics, organizes these groups hierarchically, and assigns causal local windows while preserving the standard flat MHA output interface. In 4096- token seed-0 experiments, several AAH-style local-allocation variants achieve lower validation loss than pure full attention. Short-budget ablations show that stable local allocation and head-window assignment structure matter, while fixed/local controls can be competitive with adaptive hierarchy. We interpret AAH as a structured head-wise context-allocation mechanism for quality and analysis, with Attention Coverage Ratio (ACR) reported as a selected-window routing diagnostic

</details>


### [71] [Hallucination as a Feature, not a Defect: Evaluating a multi-agent architecture to transform speculative language-model outputs into testable scientific hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19206)
*Nicolas Rodriguez-Alvarez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Rust的多智能体编排系统，通过“叙事白日梦”与“执行控制”的对比来激发LLM的创造性假设生成，同时用低熵语义瓶颈和网络搜索评估来约束幻觉。实验表明，该系统在假设需满足强物理/经验/制度约束时优势明显，但整体并不优于简单的自我反思方法，直接提示效果最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代LLM越来越强调事实准确性以抑制幻觉，这可能限制了其在研发中的组合创造力。作者希望探索如何在不完全消除幻觉的前提下，利用受控的“幻觉”来生成新颖且可行的研究假设，同时避免语义过拟合和多样性崩溃。

Method: 设计了一个Rust实现的多智能体架构，包含一个高熵生成代理（负责自由联想/白日梦）和一个基于网络搜索的评估代理（负责事实约束），两者通过低熵语义瓶颈交互，形成“认识论摩擦”循环。实验在物理和社会科学领域进行，并与直接提示、自我反思、去除语义过滤器、去除搜索基础、去除横向视角等基线进行对照和消融研究。

Result: 直接提示在多数指标上表现最差；完整系统并不普遍优于简单自我反思；不同架构在原创性、可行性、多样性和经验基础之间各有取舍。完整系统的主要优势体现在假设需要经受强物理、经验或制度约束的场景。受控的推测生成只有通过架构约束、经验基础和明确评估才能产生价值。

Conclusion: 单纯的幻觉并非有用，但通过架构设计、经验验证和显式评估来约束推测生成，可以在保持创造力的同时提升假设的可行性。该工作为如何在LLM对齐过程中平衡事实性与探索性提供了设计启示。

Abstract: Contemporary Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly aligned to suppress hallucinations, prioritizing factual retrieval over combinatorial creativity. While crucial for mitigating misinformation, this alignment may also restrict speculative Research and Development (R&D) by encouraging what this work operationally treats as semantic overfitting and diversity collapse. In this paper, we propose a Rust-based multi-agent orchestration that uses the contrast between narrative daydreaming and executive control as a functional analogy, not as a neurocognitive claim. The system instigates an Epistemological Friction loop between a high-entropy generating agent and a web-grounded evaluating agent, mediated by a low-entropy semantic bottleneck intended to reduce noise and repetition. Initial experiments generated diverse, viability-rated hypotheses across physical and social-science domains. We additionally report an exploratory paired baseline and ablation study comparing the full system against direct prompting, self-reflection, removal of the semantic filter, removal of search grounding, and removal of lateral lenses. The results place direct prompting among the weakest conditions across most observed metrics, but they do not show a general superiority of the full system over simple self-reflection. Instead, they suggest that each architecture shifts the balance between originality, feasibility, diversity, and empirical grounding in different ways, and that the full system provides its main advantages when hypotheses must survive strong physical, empirical, or institutional constraints. These findings do not show that hallucination is useful in isolation; they suggest that speculative generation gains value only when constrained by architecture, empirical grounding, and explicit evaluation.

</details>


### [72] [Compliance, Capability, and Conflict: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs under System Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19207)
*Juan Yeo,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VSysBench is a new benchmark for evaluating how well Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) adhere to system messages in multimodal contexts. It categorizes constraints into 5 main and 22 sub-categories, jointly measures constraint compliance and answer correctness using JSR and CCS, and reveals that system messages significantly degrade base accuracy, open-weight models fail under user conflict, and vision-grounded constraints are hardest.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks either evaluate constraints only in text or embed them into user turns, leaving system-message adherence in multimodal contexts unmeasured. It is also unclear whether compliance degrades foundational vision-language capabilities.

Method: VSysBench is built on MMVet-v2, organizing constraints into 5 main categories and 22 sub-categories covering textual directives in visual contexts to fully vision-grounded ones, each with a misaligned counterpart. Responses are scored via Joint Satisfaction Rate (JSR) for constraint compliance and answer correctness, and Cross-Constraint Sensitivity (CCS) for detecting sensitivity to misaligned constraints.

Result: Across 16 MLLMs, system messages substantially erode base task accuracy; compliance collapses under user conflict for open-weight models while remaining stable for top proprietary ones; vision-grounded constraints are the hardest category for every model.

Conclusion: System messages in MLLMs come at a cost to foundational vision-language capabilities, and current models vary widely in their ability to balance compliance with correctness, especially under conflicting instructions and vision-grounded constraints.

Abstract: Production deployments of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) increasingly rely on system messages to govern model behavior. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate constraints in text only or embed them into the user turn, leaving system-message adherence in multimodal contexts largely unmeasured; they also leave open whether compliance comes at the cost of foundational vision-language capabilities. We introduce VSysBench, a benchmark built on MMVet-v2 that organizes constraints into 5 main categories and 22 sub-categories, ranging from textual directives in visual contexts to fully vision-grounded ones, each paired with a misaligned counterpart that stress-tests the instructional hierarchy. VSysBench scores each response jointly along two axes, constraint compliance and answer correctness, via the Joint Satisfaction Rate (JSR) and Cross-Constraint Sensitivity (CCS). Across 16 MLLMs, we find that imposing system messages substantially erodes base task accuracy, that compliance collapses under user conflict for open-weight models while remaining stable for top proprietary ones, and that vision-grounded constraints are the hardest category for every model.

</details>


### [73] [When Irrelevant Text Matters: Affine Margin Shifts in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19208)
*Yinfeng Wang,Zhiyuan Yao,Zheren Fu,Lei Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在面对任务无关的辅助文本上下文时的表现。通过二元视觉判断框架中的受控干预，发现无关文本会一致地偏置模型预测。进一步利用决策边界（对数概率差）分析，揭示上下文条件边界与无上下文边界之间存在稳健的仿射变换关系，表明无关上下文并非随机噪声而是可估计的偏好扭曲。仿射参数可解释为视觉承诺保持度和方向性答案偏置，为MLLMs的噪声鲁棒性研究提供了诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型经常面临辅助文本上下文，但任务无关的上下文对视觉任务的影响尚未被充分探索。理解这种影响对于提升模型在嘈杂环境下的鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 将任务无关上下文的影响建模为二元视觉判断框架中的受控干预：保持提示结构不变，改变辅助输入（无关文本）。定义决策边界为二元候选答案的对数概率差，分析上下文条件边界与无上下文边界之间的关系。

Result: 无关文本一致地偏置模型预测；上下文条件边界与无上下文边界之间存在稳健的仿射变换关系（线性变换+偏移），表明无关上下文是一种可估计的偏好扭曲而非随机噪声；仿射参数可解释为视觉承诺保持度（斜率）和方向性答案偏置（截距）。

Conclusion: 提供了一个基于决策边界的诊断视角来理解MLLMs中无关上下文的影响，为未来研究嘈杂上下文鲁棒性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are frequently exposed to auxiliary textual context, the impact of which on visually grounded tasks remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate the influence of task-irrelevant context by formulating it as a controlled intervention within a binary visual judgment framework. By maintaining an invariant prompt structure while varying auxiliary inputs, we observe that irrelevant text consistently biases model predictions across diverse benchmarks. To move beyond performance metrics, we characterize this sensitivity through a decision margin defined by the log-probability difference between binary candidates. Our analysis reveals a robust geometric regularity: contextconditioned margins follow a consistent affine transformation of their context-free counterparts. This finding demonstrates that irrelevant context does not manifest as unstructured stochastic noise but as a estimable distortion of model preference. We further interpret the fitted affine parameters as metrics for visual commitment preservation and directional answer bias. These findings provide a margin-level diagnostic view of irrelevant-context effects in MLLMs and offer a basis for future studies on noisy-context robustness

</details>


### [74] [Represented but Ignored: A Causal Account of Prosodic Underuse in Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19211)
*Linkai Peng,Baorian Nuchged*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种针对音频大语言模型的阶段特定探针阶梯，用于定位其在韵律理解中的失败模式。研究发现，韵律信息通常在音频路径中得以保留，并在后期LLM状态中可解码，但仅在模型的最终响应中部分表达。通过目标隐藏状态干预，单次编辑即可驱动模型向被抑制的韵律决策方向转变，表明瓶颈不在于感知韵律，而在于使用韵律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型应支持富有表现力的语音理解，不仅转录内容，还需解释说话方式（韵律）。但行为评估无法揭示模型在韵律输入上失败的原因（是声学信息丢失、内部解释错误，还是未能利用已有表示），因此需要定位具体失败模式。

Method: 提出阶段特定探针阶梯（stage-specific probe ladder），在四种仅进行理解的音频LLM上，通过探针分析韵律信息在不同阶段的保留与可解码性，并采用目标隐藏状态干预验证潜在表示的因果作用。

Result: 韵律信息在音频路径中保留并在后期LLM状态中可解码，但最终响应中仅部分表达。隐藏状态干预可单向驱动模型向被抑制的韵律决策方向偏移，且恢复信号可通过小子空间表达，部分高归因特征与声学线索对齐。

Conclusion: 模型能够感知并正确表征韵律线索，但仍可能无法在答案中表达它。瓶颈不在于感知韵律，而在于使用韵律。

Abstract: Human speech is richly expressive, with prosody carrying linguistic and emotional information beyond the lexical content. A capable large audio-language model (audio-LLM) should therefore support expressive speech understanding, not only transcribing what was said but also interpreting how it was said. Yet behavioral evaluations alone cannot reveal why a model fails on prosodic input. An error may reflect loss of acoustic information, incorrect internal interpretation, or failure to use a representation that is already available inside the model. We introduce a stage-specific probe ladder for localizing these failure modes in audio-LLMs. Across four understanding-only audio-LLMs, prosodic information is usually preserved in the audio path and decodable in late LLM states. Yet it is only partially expressed in the model's final response. We test the causal status of this latent representation with targeted hidden-state interventions. Every intervention shifts the answer distribution in the predicted direction, and in most model--task cells a single edit at the relevant layer is sufficient to drive the model toward the suppressed prosodic decision, though this recovery is directional rather than a selective restoration of the correct class. Feature-level analysis further suggests that this recoverable signal can be expressed through a small subspace. Some of the highest-attribution features in this analysis align with acoustic cues known to carry prosodic information. Within the matched-content contrasts we test, these results locate the recurring bottleneck not in perceiving prosody but in using it. Models that hear and correctly represent a prosodic cue can still fail to express it in their answers.

</details>


### [75] [NepOOC-M: Bilingual Nepali-English Benchmark and Comparative Analysis of Multimodal Architectures for OOC Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19212)
*Sanjeev Khatiwada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NepOOC 是首个面向尼泊尔语（以尼泊尔语为主的多语言）的上下文外（OOC） misinformation 基准数据集，包含 1,090 个图文对（545 真实、545 OOC），覆盖五种虚假叙事类型。实验表明，在当前数据规模下，纯文本模型（mBERT）即可达到与最佳多模态系统相当的 94.65% Macro-F1，而纯图像模型仅接近随机水平；数据集规模扩展比架构创新或区域专业化更能直接提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文外（OOC） misinformation 通过将真实图片与误导性标题配对来构造虚假叙事，无需篡改图像本身，因此检测问题从图像取证转变为多模态对齐。尽管此类 misinformation 在尼泊尔非常普遍且危害严重，但此前没有任何公开的尼泊尔语 OOC 基准数据集，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了 NepOOC 数据集，包含 1,090 个图文对（545 真实、545 OOC），由五位注释者标注了五种 typology（虚构、错误配文、时间错配、地理错配、身份错配），注释者间一致性 kappa=0.84。系统评估了五种多模态架构（如 ResNet-50+mBERT）以及纯文本（mBERT）和纯图像基线模型，并进行了训练集规模扩展实验。

Result: 纯文本 mBERT 模型取得 94.65±0.20% Macro-F1，与最佳多模态系统 ResNet-50+mBERT（94.65±0.20%）统计等价（McNemar 中位 p=1.000，0/5 种子在 α=0.05 下显著）。纯图像模型性能接近随机（33-50%）。训练规模扩展实验表明，扩大数据集比增加架构复杂度或区域专门化更能直接提升性能。

Conclusion: 在当前 NepOOC 数据集规模下，标题语义已足以取得强性能，多模态信息并未带来显著增益；图像模型几乎无效。未来工作的更有效方向是扩展数据集规模，而非追求更复杂的架构或区域特化模型。NepOOC 为尼泊尔语 OOC misinformation 检测研究提供了首个公开基准。

Abstract: Out-of-context (OOC) misinformation pairs authentic images with misleading captions to construct false narratives without image manipulation, making detection a problem of multimodal alignment rather than image forensics. Despite the prevalence and consequences of OOC misinformation in Nepal, no public benchmark exists for Nepali. We introduce NepOOC, the first publicly available Nepali-dominant multilingual OOC benchmark, comprising 1,090 image-caption pairs (545 pristine, 545 OOC) annotated across five typologies (fabricated, miscaptioned, temporal mismatch, geographic mismatch, identity mismatch) with inter-annotator agreement kappa = 0.84. Systematic evaluation of five multimodal architectures alongside text-only and image-only baselines reveals that caption semantics appear sufficient for strong performance at the current dataset scale. A text-only mBERT model achieves 94.65+/-0.20% Macro-F1, statistically equivalent to the best multimodal system (ResNet-50+mBERT, 94.65+/-0.20%; McNemar median p = 1.000, 0/5 seeds significant at alpha = 0.05). Image-only models perform near chance (33-50%), while training-size scaling suggests that dataset expansion is a more direct path to progress than architectural sophistication or regional specialisation.

</details>


### [76] [Time-Series Retrieval for Grounding Multimodal Language Models in Remaining Useful Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19218)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计，通过时间序列检索（RAG）从训练集中检索历史相似退化片段，与测试轨迹一起转化为视觉对比图，再由MLLM处理。在C-MAPSS FD001上评估，发现检索一致提升预测性能，但收益依赖于模型容量，表明时间序列RAG对多模态预后推理有潜力但仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型和智能体AI系统在领域特定维护和预测任务中的有效性，特别是多模态大语言模型在剩余使用寿命估计中的潜力，以及如何通过时间序列检索增强其性能。

Method: 提出一个框架：从训练集中检索与测试轨迹历史相似的退化片段，将它们与测试轨迹一起转化为视觉对比图，通过结构化多模态提示输入多模态大语言模型进行RUL预测。在C-MAPSS FD001数据集上重复实验，比较基于检索的推理与基于随机参考选择的非检索基线。

Result: 时间序列检索一致地改善了多模态大语言模型的RUL预测，得到更低的误差和更稳定的性能；但收益幅度取决于模型容量，检索在底层MLLM能够利用检索证据时最有效。

Conclusion: 时间序列RAG是改善多模态预后推理的有前景机制，但也突显了当前MLLM在RUL估计中的局限性，在实际PHM场景中仍需改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic AI systems are increasingly being explored for domain-specific maintenance and prognostics tasks, raising the question of whether they can effectively support prognostics and health management (PHM). In this paper, we investigate remaining useful life (RUL) estimation with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) grounded through time-series retrieval. We propose a framework in which historically similar degradation segments are retrieved from the training set and, together with the test trajectory, transformed into a visual comparison artifact that is processed by the MLLM through a structured multimodal prompt. The approach is evaluated on the FD001 partition of the C-MAPSS benchmark under repeated experiments comparing retrieval-based inference against a non-retrieval baseline based on random reference selection. The results show that time-series retrieval consistently improves MLLM-based RUL prediction across the evaluated models, yielding lower error and more stable performance. At the same time, the magnitude of the benefit depends on model capacity, indicating that retrieval is most effective when the underlying MLLM is able to exploit the retrieved evidence. Overall, the study shows that time-series RAG is a promising mechanism for improving multimodal prognostic reasoning, while also highlighting the current limitations of MLLM-based RUL estimation in practical PHM settings.

</details>


### [77] [Can Conversational AI loosen Us-Versus-Them Boundaries? The Effects of Common, Dual, and Separate Identity Framings on Pro-Immigrant Intergroup Helping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19220)
*Oluwadamilola Jeboda,John F. Dovidio,Jonas R. Kunst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，与GPT-4o进行五轮对话，通过共同内群体身份或双重身份框架来讨论Latine移民，能降低多数群体（非Latine白人）对移民的“分离归类”，并提高“双重归类”，进而增强行动意愿，但认知上的重新归类并未直接转化为更积极的行为或信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移民问题加剧群体间紧张，传统偏见干预措施难以规模化且受政策限制，亟需可扩展的替代方案。本文探索对话式AI能否通过语言框架改变多数群体对移民的认知分类，从而缓解“我们-他们”边界。

Method: 基于共同内群体身份模型，进行预注册实验。招募658名具有全国配额代表性的非Latine白人美国成年人，与GPT-4o完成五轮对话。对话条件包括：共同内群体身份（共享美国人身份）、双重身份（既是Latine也是美国人）、分离身份（明确文化边界）、以及无关话题的控制条件。测量分类认知、行为意愿、亲多样性信念，并进行语义相似性分析和路径模型检验。

Result: 共同内群体身份和双重身份对话降低了分离归类；双重身份对话提高了双重归类。对行为和信念的直接效应不显著，但超然身份条件（共同内群体和双重身份）显著提高了行动意愿。路径模型显示两种条件通过降低分离归类间接提升行动意愿。语义分析证实对话遵循指定叙事，参与者语言趋同程度与行动意愿相关。效应在调节变量（认知闭合需求、开放性、政治取向）上基本一致。

Conclusion: 简短的AI对话能松动“我们-他们”的群体边界，但认知重新归类与行为改变之间存在差距。AI对话作为一种可扩展的偏见干预手段具有潜力，但需进一步弥合认知到行为的转化鸿沟。

Abstract: Rising immigration has intensified intergroup tensions in many countries. Traditional bias-reduction programs remain difficult to scale and increasingly constrained by U.S. policy. This preregistered experiment tested whether conversational AI can shift how majority-group members categorize and relate to Latine immigrants. Drawing on the common ingroup identity model, a quota-representative national sample of 658 non-Latine White U.S. adults completed five rounds of dialogue with a LLM (GPT-4o). The model was instructed to frame Latine immigrants in terms of a common ingroup identity (a shared American identity), a dual identity (both Latine and American), or a separate identity (distinct cultural boundaries), or to discuss an unrelated topic in a control condition. The manipulations altered categorization: relative to control, common ingroup identity and dual identity conversations lowered separate categorization, and dual identity conversations raised dual categorization. Although direct effects on behavior and pro-diversity beliefs were nonsignificant, willingness to act was significantly higher in the conditions emphasizing a superordinate identity (common ingroup and dual identity). A path model further revealed indirect associations: both conditions reduced separate categorization, which in turn correlated with greater willingness to act. Semantic similarity analyses of the transcripts confirmed that conversations tracked their assigned narratives; participants' convergence with shared-identity language related positively, and with separate-identity language negatively, to willingness to act. These effects were largely consistent across moderators (need for closure, openness to experience, and political orientation). The findings show that brief AI conversations can loosen us-versus-them boundaries while underscoring the gap between cognitive recategorization and behavior.

</details>


### [78] [A Speech Corpus for Mizo Automatic Speech Recognition: Whisper and SraVaani 1.0 Fine-Tuning with Morphology-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19361)
*Priyankoo Sarmah,Sanasam Ranbir Singh,Lalhmingmawia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低资源语言Mizo上开发自动语音识别（ASR）系统。通过收集17.62小时的语音数据，并使用三种Whisper多语言模型和SraVaani 1.0 Indic多语言模型进行微调。Whisper-large-v3取得了最低的传统词错误率（WER）18.08%，而形态感知评估的WER为7.22%。SraVaani 1.0的零样本评估WER为58.27%，微调后传统WER降至29.45%，形态感知WER降至17.93%。结果表明Whisper模型即使在未见过的语言上也能实现较低的WER，而SraVaani 1.0通过微调性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mizo是一种低资源语言，缺乏足够的语音识别研究。开发其ASR系统有助于保护语言文化并提升语音技术的覆盖范围。

Method: 收集17.62小时Mizo语音数据并进行整理，使用三种Whisper多语言模型（包括Whisper-large-v3）和SraVaani 1.0 Indic多语言模型进行微调。采用传统WER和形态感知WER两种评估指标。

Result: Whisper-large-v3传统WER为18.08%，形态感知WER为7.22%。SraVaani 1.0零样本WER为58.27%，微调后传统WER降至29.45%，形态感知WER降至17.93%。

Conclusion: Whisper模型能够有效适应未见过的低资源语言，取得较低WER。SraVaani 1.0虽然原本支持Mizo，但使用精心整理的Mizo语音数据进行微调能大幅提升性能。

Abstract: This study reports the development of an Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) system in Mizo, a low-resource language. The development included collecting 17.62 hours of speech data, curating it, and fine-tuning the Mizo ASR system with three Whisper multilingual models and with the SraVaani 1.0 Indic multilingual model. Whisper-large-v3 achieved the lowest conventional WER (18.08%), while morphology-aware evaluation yielded a WER of 7.22%. Zero-shot evaluation of the SraVaani 1.0 Indic multilingual model yielded a WER of 58.27%, while Mizo-specific fine-tuning reduced the conventional WER to 29.45% and the morphology-aware WER to 17.93%. The results demonstrate that the Whisper model can achieve a substantially low WER, even when adapted to an unseen language. In contrast, SraVaani 1.0 supports the Mizo language in its multilingual model; however, fine-tuning with carefully curated Mizo speech data substantially improves its performance.

</details>


### [79] [Linguistic Holonomy and Statistical Watermarks: Inner Geometry of Meaning-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19369)
*Daniele Corradetti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了语言模型统计水印的鲁棒性评价指标，指出传统基于端点语义相似度的度量无法捕捉水印在意义保持变换链中的消退。通过引入“语言学回路”形式体系，作者证明了变换链的不变量可分解为端点部分和初始状态稳定子中的和乐（holonomy），后者被语义相似度忽略；并推导出水印残留统计量与完整存活位置数成正比，进而得到独立编辑下的衰减律 ρ^{h+1}。一个反直觉的发现是：在相同保留率下，残留信号可能相差数倍甚至为零，完全取决于编辑的具体位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印鲁棒性评估通常只考虑原始文本与改写文本之间的语义相似度（即端点度量），但作者认为这种度量无法反映水印在多次意义保持变换过程中的实际消退情况。需要更精确的理论框架来描述水印的退化机制。

Method: 作者借鉴语言学回路（linguistic loops）的形式体系，将意义保持变换链的不变量分解为端点部分和稳定子中的和乐（holonomy），并将回路旋转解释为嵌入空间单位球面上的平行输运，从而将Wilson环类比提升为定理。在检测器端，推导了残留统计量与完整存活位置数之间的精确比例关系，并由此得到独立编辑假设下的衰减律 ρ^{h+1}。

Result: 理论推导表明，端点语义相似度无法检测到变换链中稳定子部分的和乐效应；残留信号衰减律为 ρ^{h+1}；更重要的是，在相同保留率下，残留信号可以是原始的一半、四分之一或完全为零，取决于编辑落在哪些位置，这一结论通过数值实验验证到三位小数精度。

Conclusion: 水印鲁棒性的评估不能仅依赖端点语义相似度，必须考虑变换链的整体几何结构。本文为理解语言模型水印在意义保持攻击下的行为提供了更精确的理论工具，并揭示了编辑位置对残留信号的决定性影响。

Abstract: Statistical watermarks for language models live in the freedom of the signifier: they choose among tokens that are nearly equivalent in meaning, and they are therefore eroded by exactly those transformations which move the form of a text while leaving its content in place. The literature measures such transformations by their endpoint, through the semantic similarity between the original and the rewritten text. We show that the endpoint is the wrong statistic. Adapting the formalism of linguistic loops, we prove that the invariant of a chain of meaning-preserving transformations factorises canonically into an endpoint part and a holonomy in the stabiliser of the initial state, the second of which the semantic deficit cannot see; the loop rotation is parallel transport on the unit sphere of the embedding space, so that the analogy with the Wilson loop becomes a theorem rather than a figure of speech. On the side of the detector we prove an exact identity: the residual statistic is proportional to the number of positions whose seeding window survived intact, from which the decay law $ρ^{h+1}$ follows as the independent-edit corollary. The identity has a disconcerting consequence, which we confirm to three decimal places: at one and the same retention rate the surviving signal may be one half of the original, one quarter of it, or exactly nothing, according only to where the edits fall.

</details>


### [80] [Are LLMs becoming similarly creative? Evidence from three years of models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19437)
*Nirav Patel,Josiah Crossman,Eva Aggarwal,Emily Wenger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了三年间多个LLM版本在开放式任务上的创造力表现，发现模型输出的多样性随时间显著下降，表明不同模型的创造性内容趋于同质化，这可能削弱人机协作中的人类能动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多关注可验证答案的任务，但对开放式任务（如创意生成）中LLM性能的演化知之甚少。随着LLM越来越多地支持人类创意工作，理解其在开放式任务上的趋势至关重要。

Method: 使用句嵌入相似度方法，分析LLM对Infinity-Chat100（真实开放式用户查询）和Alternate Uses Task（心理测量学创造力评估）的响应，跨越三年的模型发布。

Result: 发现模型输出多样性在统计上显著下降，不同模型的创造性内容趋于收敛。

Conclusion: 若该趋势持续，LLM驱动的同质化可能逐渐削弱人机协同创作中的人类能动性，需谨慎考虑LLM在人类创造过程中的角色。

Abstract: Many benchmarks track Large Language Model (LLM) performance on tasks with verifiable answers, but less is known about how LLM performance is evolving on open-ended tasks, where creativity, originality and diversity may matter as much as quality. As LLMs increasingly support human ideation and creative work, understanding trends in LLM performance on open-ended tasks is critical. This paper presents a preliminary analysis of LLM creative outputs spanning three years of model releases, examining model responses to Infinity-Chat100, a real-world collection of open-ended user queries, and the Alternate Uses Task, an established psychometric creativity assessment. Using sentence-embedding similarity, we examine trends in LLM responses to these prompts. Our findings show a statistically significant decrease in model output diversity over time, suggesting that LLM outputs may be converging in creative substance across models. If this trend persists, LLM-driven homogenization may progressively diminish human agency in human-AI co-creative work, demanding careful consideration of LLMs' role in the human creative process.

</details>


### [81] [SynFlow: A Multidimensional Diachronic Semantic Analysis Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19472)
*Bach Phan-Tat,Kris Heylen,Dirk Geeraerts,Stefano De Pascale,Dirk Speelman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynFlow是一个开源工具包，用于对词汇用法进行多维度历时分析，覆盖依存共现、形态特征、构式配置及框架语义等维度，支持距离测量、值级分解、统计检验和增量聚类。通过德语形容词viral的案例研究和SemEval-2020 Task 1的实验，展示了该方法在词汇语义变化检测中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的词汇语义变化（LSC）研究主要使用向量空间表示，但难以揭示哪些具体用法维度发生了变化。历时语料库研究虽能考察句法行为、形态和构式模式等可解释维度，却通常采用分离的分析流程。因此需要一种统一的多维度分析工具包。

Method: SynFlow将语言观察转化为时期特定的分布，对依存共现、形态特征、构式配置和框架语义等维度应用统一的工作流程。支持不同的距离测量方法、值级分解、统计检验以及词汇填充物的增量聚类。

Result: 通过德语形容词viral的定性案例研究，展示了单一语义发展如何在句法、词汇、构式和形态等多个维度上得到反映。此外，报告了在SemEval-2020 Task 1上先前发表的结果，以衡量这些表示相对于现有词汇语义变化检测系统的性能。

Conclusion: SynFlow提供了一种可解释的多维度历时分析方法，弥补了向量空间表示和传统历时语料库研究之间的差距，能够更全面地揭示词汇语义变化的多个维度表现。

Abstract: Lexical semantic change (LSC) is commonly modelled through vector-space representations, but these approaches often provide limited insight into which aspects of usage are changing. Diachronic corpus research instead examines interpretable dimensions such as syntactic behaviour, morphology, and constructional patterns, but typically through separate analytical workflows. We present SynFlow, an open-source toolkit for multidimensional diachronic analysis of linguistic usage. SynFlow converts linguistic observations into period-specific distributions and applies a shared workflow across dependency-based co-occurrences, morphological features, constructional configurations, and externally derived representations such as Frame Semantics. It supports different distance measures, together with value-level decomposition, statistical testing, and incremental clustering of lexical fillers. We demonstrate SynFlow through a qualitative case study of the German adjective viral, showing how a single semantic development is reflected across syntactic, lexical, constructional, and morphological dimensions. We further report previously published results on SemEval-2020 Task 1 to situate the performance of these representations relative to existing lexical semantic change detection systems.

</details>


### [82] [Hear2Act: Benchmarking When Prosody Should Change What an Assistant Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19515)
*Xinyi Liu,Hooshang Nayyeri,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Emine Yilmaz,JK Kim,Yifei Zhang,Charith Peris,Hari Thadakamalla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hear2Act，一个统一的评估协议，用于测试文本和语音助手能否利用韵律线索影响任务导向对话的决策。通过480个场景，对比显式词汇传达与韵律传达的隐藏用户关切，发现音频LLM能从语音中恢复信息，但若不经过显式的中间表征（如文本化），则难以可靠地利用韵律信息改善行动选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试将韵律感知、回应恰当性和任务导向对话分开评估，难以检验韵律证据是否真正改变下游决策。因此需要统一的评估协议来度量语音助手是否以及如何利用韵律线索。

Method: 构建Hear2Act协议，包含480个基于人物设定的场景，每个场景固定任务和用户需求，但通过两种方式（显式词汇或主要依靠韵律）传达相同的隐藏关切。评估模型在三种条件下（仅文本、音频+文本、已知关切状态）的决策表现。

Result: 在韵律中介的反馈中，添加音频仅将最优解率从14.6%提升到15.3%；但若模型从音频推断关切状态并以文本表示再用于行动选择，最优解率升至39.6%，接近已知真实状态下的40.7%。在显式词汇反馈下，这些差异基本消失。

Conclusion: 韵律在词汇证据不足时发挥重要作用；当前音频LLM能够从语音中恢复信息，但若没有显式的中间表征（如文本化的关切状态），则无法可靠地将韵律信息转化为行动改进。

Abstract: Prosodic cues can convey task-relevant information that alters the trajectory and outcome of a task-oriented dialogue, even when the words themselves remain unchanged. Yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate prosodic perception, response appropriateness, and task-oriented dialogue in isolation, making it difficult to test whether prosodic evidence changes downstream decisions. We introduce Hear2Act, a unified evaluation protocol for text and spoken assistants with 480 persona-grounded scenarios, hidden user concerns, and objectively verifiable outcomes. For each scenario, we keep the task and user needs fixed while varying whether the same concern is conveyed explicitly in words or primarily through prosody, and evaluate decisions under transcript, audio, and concern-state access.
  Using Hear2Act, we evaluate two audio-capable LLMs. Under Prosody-mediated feedback, adding audio to the transcript changes the average optimal-solution rate only from 14.6% to 15.3%. In contrast, when models infer the concern status from audio, represent it in text, and use it for next-action selection, the rate rises to 39.6%, close to 40.7% with the ground-truth state. This contrast, however, largely disappears under Explicit lexical feedback, where the concern is verbally mentioned in the utterance. Together, these results show that prosody matters when lexical evidence is insufficient, and that audio-capable LLMs can recover information from speech but do not reliably carry it into action without an explicit intermediate representation.

</details>


### [83] [Automated Summarization of Financial News Using Large Language Models and Retrieval-Augmented Generation: An Early Empirical Study (Fall 2023)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19526)
*Pranav Chandaliya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探索了使用大型语言模型（LLMs）自动化金融新闻摘要的可行性，构建了一个从News API、Wikipedia和Yahoo Finance获取数据的管道，将股票数据转换为自然语言叙述，并测试了多种摘要方法（Summarize Chains、RAG）和模型（Falcon-7B、DistilBART、BART-Large、GPT），最终发现Falcon-7B结合Summarize Chains效果最佳，同时揭示了RAG在小模型中导致重复和幻觉的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融新闻数量庞大，分析师和投资者手动阅读和综合数百篇公司特定文章不切实际，但遗漏关键信息可能直接影响投资决策。因此，需要自动化且可靠的新闻摘要工具。

Method: 构建了一个管道：从News API获取新闻，从Wikipedia获取公司背景，从Yahoo Finance获取股票价格数据。由于LLM无法直接处理数值表格，开发了一个模板将股票数据转换为自然语言叙述。测试了两种摘要方法（Summarize Chains和基于FAISS的检索增强生成RAG），使用了三个开源模型（Falcon-7B-Instruct、DistilBART-CNN-12-6、BART-Large-XSum）处理新闻，以及GPT（text-davinci-003）处理股票摘要。还构建了一个Streamlit仪表盘用于交互式股票可视化。

Result: Falcon-7B结合Summarize Chains效果最佳，能准确且连贯地覆盖所有新闻事件。RAG理论上有前景，但在Falcon中导致严重重复，在BART-Large中当k值较大时产生幻觉。两种基于LLM的方法在ROUGE-1指标上均优于简单的Lead-3基线。

Conclusion: 该工作于2023年秋季完成，早于RAG金融工具的普及。论文记录的错误模式（尤其是小模型在RAG下的幻觉问题）至今仍有参考价值。

Abstract: Stock market analysts and investors face a daily challenge: too much financial news, too little time. Manually reading and synthesizing hundreds of company-specific articles is impractical, yet missing key information can directly affect investment decisions. This project, conducted at George Washington University in Fall 2023, explores whether Large Language Models can automate this process reliably. We built a pipeline that pulls news articles from the News API, company background from Wikipedia, and stock price data from Yahoo Finance for ten major companies (AAPL, MSFT, GOOGL, AMZN, META, TSLA, JPM, NVDA, WMT, DIS). Because LLMs cannot directly process numerical tables, we developed a simple but effective template that converts stock data into natural language narratives. We then tested two summarization approaches (Summarize Chains and Retrieval-Augmented Generation with FAISS) across three open-source models (Falcon-7B-Instruct, DistilBART-CNN-12-6, BART-Large-XSum) for news, and GPT (text-davinci-003) for stock summaries. Falcon-7B with Summarize Chains gave the best results, covering all news events accurately and coherently. RAG, while promising in theory, caused severe repetition in Falcon and hallucinated facts in BART-Large when k was large. Both LLM-based approaches outperformed a simple Lead-3 baseline on ROUGE-1. We also built a Streamlit dashboard for interactive stock visualization. The work was done in Fall 2023, before RAG-based financial tools became widespread, and the failure modes we document, particularly hallucination under RAG in smaller models, remain relevant today.

</details>


### [84] [When Machines Speak: A Unified Generative Framework for Integrating Machine-Native Symbols into Pretrained Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19529)
*Su Yan,Rakesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniLang是一个统一的生成式框架，通过扩展预训练大语言模型的词表和嵌入空间，将机器原生符号（如离散的序列推荐和司法先例预测中的结构化表示）与自然语言令牌平等对待，在单一自回归目标下联合建模和生成，从而弥合了语言建模与结构化预测之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统常使用机器原生符号（非自然语言）表示实体、行为和结构化信息，但这些符号位于预训练LLM的语言词表空间之外，导致语言建模与结构化预测之间存在根本性割裂。现有方法要么需要将符号转述为自然语言，要么依赖任务特定架构，效率低下且不灵活。

Method: UniLang通过扩展LLM的词表和嵌入空间，将机器原生表示作为第一类生成单元融入模型，使文本令牌与符号令牌可在同一自回归目标下联合建模与生成，无需将符号转译为自然语言或设计任务特定结构。

Result: 在序列推荐和法律先例预测两个结构迥异的任务上，UniLang均持续优于强基线方法，验证了其跨领域结构化预测的有效性。

Conclusion: UniLang展示了将预训练LLM扩展至语言之外的路径，使其可作为异质机器原生表示的通用生成建模骨干，为融合符号推理和语言建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Many real-world AI systems represent entities, behaviors, and structured information using discrete machine-native symbols rather than natural language. While these representations are compact and preserve task-relevant structure, they lie outside the linguistic token space of pretrained large language models (LLMs), creating a fundamental divide between language modeling and structured prediction. We introduce UniLang, a unified generative framework that bridges this divide by extending pretrained LLMs to treat machine-native symbols as first-class generative units alongside natural-language tokens. UniLang expands the LLM's vocabulary and embedding space with grounded machine-native representations, enabling textual and symbolic tokens to be jointly modeled and generated under a single autoregressive objective. This unified interface allows pretrained LLMs to directly operate on machine-native representations without requiring them to be verbalized as natural language or relying on task-specific architectures. We evaluate UniLang on two structurally distinct tasks, sequential recommendation and legal precedent prediction, spanning different domains and types of structured prediction. Across both tasks, UniLang consistently outperforms strong baselines, demonstrating a path toward extending pretrained LLMs beyond language and using them as a common generative modeling backbone for heterogeneous machine-native representations.

</details>


### [85] [Generating Diverse Personas for User Simulators to Test Interview Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19549)
*Mikio Nakano,Kazunori Komatani,Hironori Takeuchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型自动生成用户模拟器的人物角色（personas），以减轻面试对话系统测试中手动创建大量角色的劳动负担，并通过赋予沟通风格相关的个性特征来增加用户模拟器的话语多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面试对话系统在开发测试时需要模拟各种用户行为，但手动创建大量用户角色非常耗时耗力；传统用户模拟器主要面向任务型对话系统训练，忽视了用户角色的多样性，因此需要一种自动生成多样化用户角色的方法。

Method: 提出一种基于大语言模型自动生成用户模拟器人物角色的方法，并在生成角色时赋予与沟通风格相关的个性特征，旨在提升用户模拟器沟通风格的多样性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够使用户模拟器生成具有更大变化性的话语。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了面试对话系统测试中手动创建用户角色的劳动问题，并通过个性特征增强了模拟用户的话语多样性，有望降低测试成本并提高测试覆盖度。

Abstract: This paper addresses the issue of the significant labor required to test interview dialogue systems. While interview dialogue systems are expected to be useful in various scenarios, like other dialogue systems, testing them with human users requires significant effort and cost. Therefore, testing with user simulators can be beneficial. Since most conventional user simulators have been primarily designed for training task-oriented dialogue systems, little attention has been paid to the personas of the simulated users. During development, testing interview dialogue systems requires simulating a wide range of user behaviors, but manually creating a large number of personas is labor-intensive. We propose a method that automatically generates personas for user simulators using a large language model. Furthermore, by assigning personality traits related to communication styles when generating personas, we aim to increase the diversity of communication styles in the user simulator. Experimental results show that the proposed method enables the user simulator to generate utterances with greater variation.

</details>


### [86] [Reliable Financial Named Entity Recognition under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19558)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 金融AI中的NER模型在域内置信度高但域外失效；全输出概率在域内是可靠错误检测器但域外退化，实体跨度概率和自一致性更鲁棒；选择性预测可将域内句子错误率从34.3%降至2%以下，但在极端社交媒体域外条件下无法恢复可用干净子集；建议采用分层部署策略，先检测严重分布漂移再应用置信度门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准F1分数无法指示当输入分布变化时哪些预测可以安全自动化，金融AI系统常在一个文本域训练信息提取器却跨域部署（如SEC文件、新闻、用户生成内容），需要研究置信度估计和选择性预测在跨域条件下的可靠性。

Method: 构建三层压力测试（SEC文件、金融新闻、社交媒体），使用BERT标注器和LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B模型，评估五种推理时置信度信号、三种训练种子和bootstrap区间，分析域内外置信度排名变化和选择性预测效果。

Result: 置信度排名在分布漂移下发生变化：全输出概率在域内最强但域外退化，实体跨度概率和自一致性更鲁棒且自一致性无需事后校准。选择性预测将域内句子错误率从34.3%降至2%以下，对金融新闻仍有效，但在极端社交媒体域外条件下无法恢复可用干净子集。

Conclusion: 建议采用分层部署策略，先在上游检测严重分布漂移，再应用预测级置信度门控，以确保金融AI系统在跨域部署时的安全自动化。

Abstract: Financial AI systems often train information extractors on one textual register and deploy them across filings, news, and user-generated content, while standard F1 scores do not indicate which predictions remain safe to automate when the input distribution changes. We study confidence estimation and selective prediction for financial named entity recognition (NER) on a three-tier stress test spanning SEC filings, financial news, and general-topic social media as an extreme out-of-domain condition. We evaluate a BERT tagger and LoRA-tuned Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B models using five inference-time confidence signals, three training seeds, and bootstrap intervals. Confidence rankings themselves change under distribution shift: whole-output probability is the strongest in-domain error detector but deteriorates out of domain, whereas entity-span probability and self-consistency are more robust; self-consistency is also better calibrated without post-hoc fitting. Abstention reduces sentence error from 34.3% to below 2% on the highest-confidence 40% of in-domain inputs and remains useful on financial news, but recovers no usefully large clean subset under the extreme social-media shift. These results motivate a staged deployment strategy that detects severe distribution shift upstream before applying prediction-level confidence gating.

</details>


### [87] [Remember, Verify, or Ask? Cross-Family Evaluation of Memory Commitment in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19564)
*Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究 LLM 代理持久记忆的“记忆-澄清边界”（MCB），即交互信息是否应持久化、仅用于当前上下文、重新验证或向用户澄清。构建了包含 140 个主要场景和 70 个对比场景的基准，评估动作标签和结构化工具调用选择。非作者标注者间一致性达 97.1%（Cohen's kappa=0.962）。实验发现模型验证变化事实比主动询问用户更可靠，少样本提示将准确率从 0.557 提升至 0.771，但澄清召回率仅 0.333；策略提示减少了错误持久化但准确率提升不显著。记忆评估必须同时测试决策声明和工具调用选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化记忆可以个性化 LLM 代理，但错误的持久更新会静默扭曲未来行为。因此需要研究如何确定交互信息应该被持久化、仅用于当前上下文、重新验证还是向用户澄清，即定义“记忆-澄清边界”。

Method: 构建 MCB 基准，包含 140 个主要场景（70 开发 + 70 保留）和 70 个对比场景；评估动作标签和结构化工具调用选择。由两名非作者独立标注 70 个保留主要和 70 个对比项（97.1% 一致，kappa=0.962），盲选第三方解决 4 处分歧。在 Claude 和 Qwen 模型上测试，使用少样本提示和策略提示等干预方法。

Result: 模型验证变化事实比主动询问用户更可靠；Qwen 在 12 个澄清项上 0 次询问，但验证了 12/18 个新鲜度项。少样本提示将准确率从 0.557 提升至 0.771（p_H=0.002），但澄清召回率仅 0.333。策略提示将错误持久化从 0.243 降至 0.100（p_H=0.038），但准确率提升不显著。标签-工具一致性：Claude 57%，Qwen 23%；Qwen 准确率从 0.557 降至 0.343（p_H=0.047）。

Conclusion: 记忆评估必须同时测试声明决策和工具调用选择，因为两者可能不一致。当前模型在主动询问用户澄清方面性能不足，少样本提示和策略提示各有优劣，未来需要更好的方法来平衡持久化与澄清。

Abstract: Persistent memory can personalize an LLM agent, but an incorrect durable update can silently distort future behavior. We study the memory-clarification boundary: whether interaction-derived information should be persisted, used only in the current context, re-verified, or clarified with the user. MCB contains 140 primary scenarios, split into 70 development and 70 held-out items, plus a separate 70-item contrast set. It evaluates both action labels and structured tool-call selection. Two non-authors independently label the 70 held-out primary and 70 contrast items (97.1% agreement, Cohen's kappa = 0.962); a blind third resolves four disagreements, replacing eight author labels by non-author majority. Across Claude and Qwen, models verify changing facts more reliably than they ask users to resolve ambiguity. Bare Qwen asks on 0/12 clarification items while verifying 12/18 freshness items. Few-shot prompting raises accuracy from 0.557 to 0.771 (paired delta = +0.214, Holm-adjusted exact McNemar p_H = 0.002), yet clarification recall remains 0.333. The policy prompt reduces erroneous persistence from 0.243 to 0.100 (p_H = 0.038), although its accuracy gain is not significant. Label-tool agreement is 57% for each Claude model and 23% for Qwen; Qwen accuracy falls from 0.557 to 0.343 (p_H = 0.047). Memory evaluation must test both stated decisions and tool-call choices.

</details>


### [88] [Forking Fast: Efficiently Estimating Uncertainty Dynamics in Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19611)
*Eric Bigelow,Amir Zur,Satchel Grant,Tal Haklay,Can Rager,Owen Lewis,Thomas McGrath,Jack Merullo,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Atticus Geiger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统计模型，通过对低采样步数下的 rollout 数据进行平滑处理，近似高采样步数下的不确定性动态，从而大幅降低 LLM 推理不确定性分析中重采样（resampling）的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重采样的分析方法需要在推理链的每个 token 或句子处重复采样，计算开销极大；亟需一种更高效的方法来刻画 LLM 推理的不确定性分布。

Method: 基于“不确定性动态在大量重采样下会收敛到稳定模式”这一发现，开发了一个统计模型，对噪声较大的低样本 rollout 数据进行平滑处理，使其逼近高样本数据下的真实不确定性动态。

Result: 实验表明，不确定性动态确实随采样次数增加而趋于稳定，噪声主要来源于采样过程而非模型对单个 token 的敏感性；所提平滑模型能显著减少所需的采样成本。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了更高效的重采样分析手段，还为理解文本生成中不确定性动态的统计模型提供了新见解。

Abstract: LLM reasoning is stochastic, and so understanding a model requires grappling with the distribution of reasoning chains that it might produce for a given question, i.e., its uncertainty. Resampling-based analyses characterize this distribution, revealing which steps of a rollout determine how the model arrives at its answer. However, a major limitation of these approaches is that resampling text sequences at every token or sentence in a reasoning chain is very costly. Our work strives to make resampling analysis more computationally efficient, while also shedding light on an important scientific question: what is the right statistical model for explaining uncertainty dynamics in text generation? We show that when resampling many reasoning chains, uncertainty dynamics converge to stable patterns, and noise is largely an artifact of sampling rather than an LLM's sensitivity to each individual token or reasoning step. We develop a statistical model for smoothing noisy low-sample rollout data to better approximate high-sample data, allowing us to significantly cut sampling costs.

</details>


### [89] [Mitigating Identity Essentialism in LLM Agents with Longitudinal Life Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19621)
*Hexi Wang,Yujia Zhou,Bangde Du,Weihang Su,Xinyuan Cao,Qingyi Pan,Qingyao Ai,Yueyue Wu,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出LifeMem，一种纵向记忆框架，用于构建更真实、动态演化的LLM社会模拟智能体。研究发现静态档案式智能体会因身份本质主义而表现出比人类更强的组间分离和组内压缩；LifeMem通过结合结构化生活事件检索与智能体特定参数化记忆，在三个LLM上显著改善了对人类数据的对齐（响应分布、整体与组内多样性、跨生命阶段个体内变化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为大规模社会模拟提供了可扩展途径，但其可信度取决于智能体的构建方式。现有方法只能部分复现群体层面的模式，常无法捕捉类人的多样性。作者分析发现，静态档案式智能体表现出比人类更强的组间分离与组内压缩，这符合身份本质主义——人口统计学标签会促使模型将群体平均倾向当作个体特质，从而同化组内响应。此局限源自两个相关因素：稀疏、静态的智能体表征，以及提示词型记忆无法持续整合经验。

Method: 借鉴互补记忆系统，提出LifeMem纵向记忆框架。它由两部分组成：一是结构化生活事件检索（外显记忆），负责提取与个体相关的生活事件；二是智能体特定参数化记忆（内隐记忆），用于经验的持续整合，弥补prompt-only记忆的不足。在Add Health与Understanding Society数据集上，用三个LLM进行实验评估。

Result: 在Add Health和Understanding Society两个数据集上与三个LLM的实验中，LifeMem相较基线在响应分布、整体及组内多样性、跨生命阶段个体内响应变化模式等方面均更好地对齐了人类数据。

Conclusion: 纵向生活事件记忆对于构建更忠实且动态演化的社会智能体具有重要价值；减少身份本质主义导致的同质化，需要更丰富、动态、能持续整合经验的智能体表征。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable approach to social simulation, but their credibility depends on how agents are constructed. Existing methods can partially reproduce population-level patterns, yet often fail to capture human-like diversity. Our analysis shows that static-profile agents exhibit stronger demographic separation and within-group compression than humans, a pattern consistent with identity essentialism: demographic labels can encourage models to treat group-average tendencies as individual traits, homogenizing responses within groups. We argue that this limitation arises from two related factors: sparse, static agent representations and the limited ability of prompt-only memory to persistently integrate experience. Inspired by complementary memory systems, we propose LifeMem, a longitudinal memory framework that combines structured life-event retrieval with agent-specific parametric memory for experience integration. Experiments on Add Health and Understanding Society with three LLMs show that LifeMem improves alignment with human data in terms of response distributions, overall and within-group diversity, and patterns of within-person response change across life stages. These findings highlight the value of longitudinal life-event memory for constructing more faithful and dynamically evolving social agents.

</details>


### [90] [ReCache: Efficient KV Cache Reuse and Compression for Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19662)
*Yichu Fang,Sitong Wei,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReCache 是一种框架，通过资源级注意力、贡献选择路由和结构语义剪枝，独立缓存工具/技能模式的 KV 状态，实现 3.655× 首令牌延迟加速、92.43% KV 内存节省和 1.423× 注意力加速，性能仅轻微下降（Inv-F1 82.3% vs 82.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体语言模型反复编码工具和技能模式，这些模式以不同组合和顺序跨请求重复出现，导致标准前缀缓存无法复用其 KV 状态，造成推理时高昂的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出 ReCache 框架：(1) 资源级注意力——消除跨资源交互，分配资源局部位置，生成组合不变的 KV 块；(2) 贡献选择路由——将资源可见性限制在选定的层-KV-头组路径上；(3) 结构语义剪枝——仅保留调用关键字段。

Result: 在 7 个公开工具/技能数据集（含资源分离测试）上，资源级注意力匹配密集调用性能（Inv-F1 82.3% vs 82.4%），提供 3.655× 首令牌延迟加速；完整框架减少 92.43% 分配的 KV 张量内存，加速注意力 1.423×。

Conclusion: 将可复用的模式编码与选择性资源访问分离，可以在有限的效果损失下大幅降低智能体推理成本。

Abstract: Agentic language models repeatedly encode tool and skill schemas that recur across requests in different combinations and orders, preventing standard prefix caching from reusing their key--value (KV) states. We introduce \textbf{ReCache}, a framework for independently caching resource representations while reducing their inference-time computational and memory overhead. Resource-wise attention removes cross-resource interactions and assigns resource-local positions, producing composition-invariant KV blocks. ReCache then restricts resource visibility to contribution-selected layer--KV-head-group routes and retains only invocation-critical fields through structural and semantic pruning. We evaluate ReCache on a benchmark assembled from seven public tool- and skill-use datasets, including resource-disjoint tests. Resource-wise attention matches dense invocation performance (82.3\% versus 82.4\% Inv-F1) while providing a 3.655$\times$ time-to-first-token speedup. The complete framework reduces allocated KV-tensor memory by 92.43\% and accelerates attention by 1.423$\times$. These results show that separating reusable schema encoding from selective resource access substantially reduces agentic inference costs with limited effectiveness loss. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/ReCache.

</details>


### [91] [The Asymmetric Harms of LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19670)
*Yuan Wu,Mairui Li,Lesia Semenova,Chudi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 压缩大语言模型虽降低了部署成本，但标准聚合指标（困惑度、准确率）掩盖了模型在知识保留、置信度和社会偏见方面的非对称行为变化：压缩不成比例地削弱头部知识、对错误答案保持高度自信，并可能隐藏跨子群的对立刻板印象偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注压缩模型的聚合性能指标（如困惑度、准确率），但这些指标无法揭示压缩带来的微观行为变化。作者认为在部署前需要更细粒度的评估，以理解压缩对知识保留、模型置信度和社会偏见的真实影响。

Method: 对3种语言模型（LLMs）应用11种不同的压缩方法，系统评估压缩对知识保留（头部知识 vs 尾部知识）、模型置信度（错误答案上的自信程度）以及社会偏见（跨人口统计子群的刻板印象偏好）的影响。

Result: 1）压缩不成比例地减少头部知识的相对保留（相较于尾部知识）；2）压缩模型在面对新丢失的知识时，仍然对其错误答案保持高度自信；3）稳定的聚合偏见分数可能掩盖了不同人口统计子群之间刻板印象偏好的显著对立性转变。

Conclusion: 压缩导致了聚合性能指标无法捕捉的非对称行为变化，强调在部署压缩模型之前需要进行细粒度评估，而不能仅依赖标准聚合指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) compression reduces deployment costs, but standard aggregate metrics like perplexity and accuracy often mask underlying behavioral shifts. In this work, we systematically evaluate 3 LLMs across 11 compression methods to investigate the effects of compression on knowledge retention, model confidence, and social bias. We find that compression disproportionately reduces the relative retention of head knowledge compared to tail knowledge. Furthermore, compressed models often remain substantially confident in their incorrect answers on newly lost knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate that stable aggregate bias scores can conceal substantial, opposing shifts in stereotypical preferences across demographic subgroups. Together, these findings reveal asymmetric behavioral changes that aggregate performance measures fail to capture, highlighting the need for granular evaluation of compressed models before deployment.

</details>


### [92] [Projector Is All You Train](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19726)
*Nyx Iskandar,Saathvik Selvan,Slater Victoroff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在适应新模态时是否需要微调语言模型主干。通过在3D MLLM上的实验，发现仅训练投影器即可达到与联合训练相当甚至更优的多模态性能，同时避免主干能力漂移，并提升约两倍的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLLM训练过程涉及同时适应语言模型主干和投影器，但这一范式是否必要尚不明确。研究者想验证仅训练投影器（而不微调主干）能否有效将MLLM扩展到新模态（如3D），并避免联合训练导致的语言模型原有能力退化。

Method: 在3D MLLM场景下进行实验，对比仅训练投影器（projector-only training）与联合训练（同时训练主干和投影器）的效果。采用相同的编码器和主干，通过3D分类、3D描述生成以及标准语言、视觉和空间推理基准进行评估，并验证不同语言模型主干下的表现。

Result: 仅训练投影器即可获得与联合训练相当甚至更强的多模态性能；联合训练会导致语言模型原有能力的非期望漂移，而仅训练投影器自然避免这一问题；此外，仅训练投影器的训练吞吐量约为联合训练的两倍。

Conclusion: 将MLLM扩展到新模态时，微调语言模型主干并非必要；仅训练投影器是一种高效且能保持主干能力的替代方案。

Abstract: The typical training process of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) involves adapting both the language model backbone and the projector between the backbone and a modality-specific encoder. We ask whether fine-tuning the backbone of an MLLM is necessary to adapt it to a new modality. Through experiments on 3D MLLMs, we find that training only the projector is sufficient to achieve strong multimodal performance relative to existing baseline models and our jointly trained MLLMs with the same encoder and backbone. We also show that joint training leads to undesirable drift in existing capabilities of the language model, which projector-only training avoids by definition. Furthermore, projector-only training has approximately twice the training sample throughput of joint training. We validate our findings across different language model backbones via 3D classification and captioning benchmarks as well as standard benchmarks evaluating language, vision, and spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [93] [One Success Isn't Reliability: Thinkingbox, a Sandbox and Benchmark for Agents in Stateful Business Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19741)
*Zhuochun Li,Youngmin Ko,Ali Keramati,Nicola Ferri,Susana Palmaz Lopez Pelaez,Liang-Chun Tsai,Calvin Wang,Mirco Milletari,Tuhin Kundu,Vadim Smolyakov,Kjartan Olafsson,Tommy Guy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Thinkingbox 是一个用于工具-智能体-用户交互的沙箱环境，支持隔离的 MCP 兼容工具会话、完整执行轨迹追踪和基于终端后端状态的结果评估。基于此沙箱构建的 Thinkingbox-bench 包含 507 个策略条件化工作流，覆盖零售、酒店、汽车保险、新银行内部 IT 和咨询 IT/HR 支持等多个场景。评测发现，最强模型 pass@1 仅 65.36%，pass^20 仅 25.25%，且大量失败轨迹看似正常终止并有有效状态变更动作，表明响应或工具调用层面的信号无法准确代理端到端任务完成度，揭示了偶尔成功与可靠完成状态化业务任务之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试多基于可执行环境（如代码修复、网页导航、API 调用等）进行评估，但完成实际业务工作不仅需要生成合理响应或有效工具调用，更要求智能体能在多轮交互中收集缺失信息、遵循领域策略、协调依赖工具、实现正确的持久状态变更且不产生副作用。现有评估信号（如响应质量或工具调用正确性）无法反映端到端任务完成的真实情况，因此需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 提出 Thinkingbox 沙箱系统，提供隔离的 MCP 兼容工具会话、完整执行轨迹记录和基于终端后端状态的结果评估。在此基础上构建 Thinkingbox-bench，包含 507 个策略条件化工作流，涵盖零售、酒店、汽车保险、新银行内部 IT、咨询 IT/HR 支持等场景。每个任务通过特定可执行检查进行评估，接受有效轨迹而拒绝错误、缺失或额外效果；指定任务额外检查最终响应的必要属性。

Result: 在多个闭源和开源模型上测试，最强模型 pass@1 为 65.36%，pass^20 仅为 25.25%。大量失败轨迹显示干净终止和有效状态变更动作，说明响应或工具调用层面的信号不能作为端到端任务完成的清晰代理。揭示出偶然找到成功轨迹与可靠完成状态化业务任务之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: Thinkingbox 和 Thinkingbox-bench 为评估智能体在真实业务场景中的端到端任务完成能力提供了有效工具，揭示了现有评估方式的不足以及当前模型在可靠完成状态化业务任务方面的局限性。项目已开源：https://github.com/microsoft/thinkingbox。

Abstract: Recent agent benchmarks increasingly ground evaluation in executable environments, from code repair to web navigation, app APIs, and function calling. Yet completing consequential work beyond code requires more than producing a plausible response or valid tool call: agents must gather missing information over multiple turns, follow domain policies, coordinate dependent tools, and realize the correct persistent state transition without collateral effects. In this paper, we introduce Thinkingbox, a sandbox for tool-agent-user interaction that provides isolated MCP-compatible tool sessions, complete execution traces, and outcome evaluation over terminal backend state. Built on this sandbox, Thinkingbox-bench contains 507 policy-conditioned workflows across numerous scenarios, including retail, hospitality, auto insurance, neobank internal IT, and consulting IT/HR support. Each attempt is evaluated by task-specific executable checks that accept valid trajectories while rejecting wrong, missing, or extra effects; designated tasks additionally check required properties of the final response. Across proprietary and open-weight models, the strongest achieves 65.36% pass@1, but only 25.25% pass^20. Moreover, many failed trials show clean termination and valid state-changing actions, showing that response or tool-call-level signals are not clear proxies for end-to-end task completion. Thinkingbox-bench reveals a large gap between occasionally finding a successful trajectory and reliably completing stateful business tasks. We release both Thinkingbox and Thinkingbox-Bench: https://github.com/microsoft/thinkingbox

</details>


### [94] [PersonalBench: Measuring the Authorship Gap in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19746)
*Yash Ganpat Sawant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PersonalBench 是一个评估推理时个性化文本生成方法的基准。它通过 LUAR（作者身份验证模型）、LLM-as-judge 和自动文体学三个独立视角评估输出是否真正模仿目标作者的写作风格。实验发现：个性化方法确实能产生作者区分度（LUAR 区分目标作者的 AUC=0.918），但所有方法输出的文本与真实作者的 LUAR 相似度在 0.484-0.508 之间，低于跨作者人类下限 0.626（上限 0.756）。LLM 自身的作者身份指纹占主导地位，生成的文本比随机人类之间的差距更大。方法之间在 LUAR 上统计无差异（跨度 0.024），尽管在 LLM 评判中看似有差异——这种不一致源于特质提取与画像提取之间的循环性。结论：推理时个性化可以调节 LLM 的风格，但无法弥合与人类作者身份之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么衡量任务准确率，要么衡量偏好对齐，而不评估模型输出是否真正像目标作者的写作。因此需要一个专门评估个性化文本生成是否真正模仿作者风格的基准。

Method: 提出 PersonalBench 基准，通过三个独立视角评估：LUAR（训练的作者身份验证模型）、LLM 作为评判者、自动文体学分析。覆盖 50 位作者、1000 次生成、两个模型家族（Qwen 3, GLM-4）。同时验证了 LUAR 在该语料库上的可靠性（单帖 AUC=0.76，多帖 AUC=0.96）。

Result: 个性化方法能产生作者区分度（LUAR 区分目标作者的 AUC=0.918），但从未跨越人机边界。所有方法的 LUAR 相似度在 0.484-0.508 之间，远低于人类下限 0.626。LLM 自身指纹主导：生成文本与任何人类作者的距离 > 随机人类之间的差异。方法间在 LUAR 上统计无差异（跨度仅 0.024），但在 LLM 评判中看似有差异，该差异源于特质提取与画像提取的循环性。

Conclusion: 推理时个性化方法确实能调节 LLM 的风格，产生作者区分度，但无法使输出达到人类作者身份的水平。PersonalBench 可作为校准标尺：个性化方法调制了 LLM 的风格，但并未弥合与人类作者身份之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Personalized text generation aims to make LLMs write in a specific individual's style, yet existing benchmarks measure task accuracy or preference alignment rather than whether the model's output actually resembles the target author's writing. We introduce PersonalBench, a benchmark that evaluates inference-time personalization methods through three independent lenses: LUAR (a trained authorship verification model), an LLM-as-judge, and automated stylometrics. Across 50 authors, 1,000 generations, and two model families (Qwen 3, GLM-4), we find that personalization methods do produce author-differentiated output (LUAR discriminates target authors within generated text at AUC=0.918) but this differentiation never crosses the human-LLM boundary. All methods achieve LUAR similarity to real authors in the range 0.484-0.508, below the cross-author human floor of 0.626 (ceiling 0.756). The LLM's own authorship fingerprint dominates: generated text is more distant from any human author than random humans are from each other. Methods are statistically indistinguishable from each other on LUAR (spread 0.024) despite appearing differentiated on the LLM judge, a discrepancy we trace to circularity between trait extraction and profile extraction. We validate that LUAR reliably measures authorship in our corpus (AUC=0.76 single-post, 0.96 multi-post). We release PersonalBench as a calibrated measuring stick: inference-time personalization modulates the LLM's style but does not bridge the gap to human authorship.

</details>


### [95] [FlashPrefill V2: Block-Sparse Prefill Attention for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19758)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashPrefill V2 将稀疏注意力从原型推进到生产级部署，通过均值校正项、与 FlashAttention-3/4 对齐的算子重设计（PackGQA 访存、warp specialization、pingpong pipelining、FP8 支持）以及原生分页 KV 缓存和连续批处理支持，在 NVIDIA H20 上实现最高 47.26x（FP8）/27.19x（BF16）的预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文建模中注意力机制的二次复杂度是预填充阶段的瓶颈，现有 FlashPrefill 虽能缓解但仍是算法原型，离生产部署仍有距离。需要将其从原型演变为实用的长上下文推理后端。

Method: 沿三个维度改进：1) 引入均值校正项抑制稀疏注意力近似误差，在极高稀疏度下仍保持低性能退化；2) 重设计稀疏注意力算子，采用 PackGQA 访存、warp specialization 和 pingpong pipelining，与 FlashAttention-3/4 实现对齐并支持 FP8 推理；3) 原生支持分页 KV 缓存和连续批处理，可直接作为 SGLang 等框架的注意力后端。

Result: 在主流推理加速卡 NVIDIA H20 上，128K 上下文长度下相较 FlashAttention-2 实现 FP8 下最高 47.26x、BF16 下最高 27.19x 加速比；FP8 下相比对齐 FA3/4 的稠密基线仍有 30.49x 加速。

Conclusion: FlashPrefill V2 成功地实现了稀疏注意力从原型到生产级部署的跨越，在保持精度可控的前提下显著提升长上下文预填充效率，可集成于现代推理框架。

Abstract: Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. Our previous work, FlashPrefill, mitigates this cost through instantaneous pattern discovery and max-based dynamic thresholding; however, it remains an algorithmic prototype that is still distant from production deployment. In this paper, we present FlashPrefill V2, which evolves FlashPrefill from a prototype toward practical long-context serving along three dimensions. First, we introduce a mean correction term that effectively suppresses the approximation error, keeping performance degradation manageable even at extreme sparsity levels. Second, we redesign the sparse attention operator with PackGQA memory access, warp specialization, and pingpong pipelining, fully aligning with the latest FlashAttention-3/4 implementations and supporting FP8 inference to meet practical quantization requirements. Third, FlashPrefill V2 natively supports paged KV cache and continuous batching, allowing integration as an attention backend in modern inference frameworks such as SGLang. Extensive evaluations on NVIDIA H20 GPUs---among the most widely deployed inference accelerators---demonstrate that FlashPrefill V2 delivers up to 47.26x and 27.19x speedups over FlashAttention-2 at 128K context length under FP8 and BF16 precision, respectively, and, in FP8, still achieves a 30.49x speedup against an FA3/4-aligned dense baseline.

</details>


### [96] [SWE-bench Science: Can Coding Agents Resolve Engineering Tasks in Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19799)
*Zhipeng Xu,Jiahao Lu,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-bench Science，一个面向科学软件工程的仓库级基准测试，包含来自20个科学领域98个GitHub仓库的119个任务。最佳代理（Claude Code + Opus-5 max）pass@1低于50%，揭示了科学软件工程中的显著挑战。识别出四种重复出现的失败机制，并通过消融实验发现科学知识并非 uniformly beneficial：良好对齐的信息可提升性能，而不当的引导则可能诱发锚定效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码代理评估主要关注总体任务成功率，对修复科学软件时失败的原因缺乏深入洞察。科学软件中的故障不仅影响程序行为，还可能损害科学结论的证据基础，因此亟需专门评估科学软件工程能力的基准测试。

Method: 构建SWE-bench Science基准，包含119个来自98个科学仓库的任务，按三种范式（Issue-driven、Expert-exploratory、Engineering-integration）组织。评估多种代码代理的表现，分析失败机制，并进行配对消融实验（移除明确科学指导但保留仓库和工程上下文）来检验科学知识的贡献。

Result: 最佳代理Claude Code with Opus-5 (max)的pass@1低于50%。识别出四种失败机制：科学知识或抽象能力不足、误导性探索或表面修复、修复覆盖不完整或系统集成失败、未能将科学知识推广到观察案例之外。消融实验表明科学知识并非 uniformly beneficial：良好对齐的信息可提升性能和token效率，而不当引导则可能诱发锚定并降低精确修复成功率。

Conclusion: SWE-bench Science为研究代码代理在科学软件工程中的能力和失败机制提供了广泛的测试平台。科学知识的价值依赖于其对齐质量——正确约束可提升修复效率，而错误引导则可能适得其反。
